# Derived coverings

Built from published coverings by the constructions described in [derived/](../derived/README.md): products over a split of the points, complement duality and one-point extensions. Each entry names every component covering used; the files are in [derived/components](../derived/components/) and every one is also on the Covering Repository (at this size or better). Rebuild any entry with `python derived/build.py v,k,t,m`. Plain coverings are the case m = t. "Status" shows the table on 2026-10-24.
